**english**　Presence-only Prediction (MaxEnt)

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 空間關系建模工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/presence-only-prediction.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/presence-only-prediction.html

**parameter**　resampling_scheme

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- resampling_scheme （可選）
- 指定將用于執行預測模型的交叉驗證的方法。 交叉驗證會在模型訓練期間排除一部分資料，并在模型訓練后使用該資料來測試模型的性能。 NONE—不會執行交叉驗證。 這是默認設置 RANDOM—點將被隨機分組，在進行交叉驗證時每組將被排除一次。 在 number_of_groups 參數中指定組的數量。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/presence-only-prediction.html#parameters

## 僅存在預測 (MaxEnt) (空間統計工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　49e61008e1c610d166-68

**english**　Presence-only Prediction (MaxEnt)

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 空間關系建模工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/presence-only-prediction.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/presence-only-prediction.html

**text**　# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Call Presence-only Prediction (MaxEnt)
arcpy.stats.PresenceOnlyPrediction(
    input_point_features=r"C:\MyData.gdb\Presence_Points",
    contains_background="PRESENCE_ONLY_POINTS",
    presence_indicator_field=None,
    explanatory_variables=None,
    distance_features=None,
    explanatory_rasters=[[r"C:\MyData.gdb\Elevation", "false"],
                         [r"C:\MyData.gdb\Canopy", "false"],
                         [r"C:\MyData.gdb\ClimacticWaterDeficit", "false"],
                         [r"C:\MyData.gdb\LandCoverClassification", "true"],
                         [r"C:\MyData.gdb\UpperSlope", "false"],
                         [r"C:\MyData.gdb\LowerSlope", "false"]],
    basis_expansion_functions="LINEAR;QUADRATIC;PRODUCT;HINGE",
    number_knots=10,
    study_area_type="CONVEX_HULL",
    study_area_polygon=None,
    spatial_thinning="THINNING",
    thinning_distance_band="500 Meters",
    number_of_iterations=10,
    relative_weight=100,
    link_function="CLOGLOG",
    presence_probability_cutoff=0.5,
    output_trained_features=r"C:\MyData.gdb\Out_Trained_Features",
    output_trained_raster=r"C:\MyData.gdb\Out_Trained_Raster",
    output_response_curve_table=r"C:\MyData.gdb\Out_Response_Curve_Table",
    output_sensitivity_table=r"C:\MyData.gdb\Out_Sensitivity_Table",
    features_to_predict=r"C:\MyData.gdb\In_Prediction_Features",
    output_pred_features=r"C:\MyData.gdb\Out_Prediction_Features",
    output_pred_raster=r"C:\MyData.gdb\Out_Prediction_Raster",
    explanatory_variable_matching=None,
    explanatory_distance_matching=None,
    explanatory_rasters_matching=[[r"C:\MyData.gdb\Prediction_Elevation", "false"],
                                  [r"C:\MyData.gdb\Prediction_Canopy", "false"],
                                  [r"C:\MyData.gdb\Prediction_ClimacticWaterDeficit", "false"],
                                  [r"C:\MyData.gdb\Prediction_LandCoverClassification", "true"],
                                  [r"C:\MyData.gdb\Prediction_UpperSlope", "false"],
                                  [r"C:\MyData.gdb\Prediction_LowerSlope", "false"]],
    allow_predictions_outside_of_data_ranges="ALLOWED",
    resampling_scheme="RANDOM",
    number_of_groups=3)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/presence-only-prediction.html#parameters

## 僅存在預測 (MaxEnt) (空間統計工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　49e61008e1c610d166-70

**english**　Presence-only Prediction (MaxEnt)

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 空間關系建模工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/presence-only-prediction.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/presence-only-prediction.html

**text**　# This example is a simple run of the tool using presence-only points and
# explanatory training rasters to train an initial model. No outputs are
# specified, as the intent is to interrogate geoprocessing messages to gain
# an initial sense of model performance.

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set the workspace and overwrite properties
arcpy.env.workspace = r"C:\MyData.gdb"
arcpy.env.overwriteOutput = True

# Set the input point feature parameters
in_point_features = "presence_observations"
contains_background = "PRESENCE_ONLY_POINTS"

# Set the explanatory Training variables, using only explanatory rasters
# Note the categorical setting for the LandCoverClassification raster
explanatory_rasters = [["Elevation", "false"],
                       ["Canopy", "false"],
                       ["ClimacticWaterDeficit", "false"],
                       ["LandCoverClassification", "true"],
                       ["UpperSlope", "false"],
                       ["LowerSlope", "false"]]

# Set basis functions, adding quadratic to use the square of each variable
basis_functions = "LINEAR;QUADRATIC"
number_knots = 10

# Set the study area
study_area_type = "CONVEX_HULL"
study_area_polygon = None

# Set cross-validation options
resampling_scheme = "RANDOM"
number_of_groups = 3

# Call the tool using the parameters defined above.
arcpy.stats.PresenceOnlyPrediction(
    input_point_features=in_point_features,
    contains_background=contains_background,
    explanatory_rasters=explanatory_rasters,
    basis_expansion_functions=basis_functions,
    study_area_type=study_area_type,
    resampling_scheme=resampling_scheme,
    number_of_groups=number_of_groups)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/presence-only-prediction.html#parameters

## 僅存在預測 (MaxEnt) (空間統計工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　49e61008e1c610d166-72

**english**　Presence-only Prediction (MaxEnt)

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 空間關系建模工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/presence-only-prediction.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/presence-only-prediction.html

**text**　# This example uses presence and background points and explanatory
# variables from rasters, fields, and distance features to train a
# model, using additional parameters to apply basis functions, use
# spatial thinning, perform cross-validation, and receive diagnostic
# training outputs.

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set the workspace and overwrite properties
arcpy.env.workspace = r"C:\MyData.gdb"
arcpy.env.overwriteOutput = True

### MODEL INPUTS ###

# Set the input point feature parameters
in_point_features = "presence_observations"
contains_background = "PRESENCE_AND_BACKGROUND_POINTS"
presence_indicator_field = "Presence"

# Set the explanatory Training variables
explanatory_fields = [["Survey_Region", "true"],
                      ["Temperature", "false"],
                      ["Humidity", "false"]]
explanatory_rasters = [["Elevation", "false"],
                       ["Canopy", "false"],
                       ["ClimacticWaterDeficit", "false"],
                       ["LandCoverClassification", "true"],
                       ["UpperSlope", "false"],
                       ["LowerSlope", "false"]]
explanatory_dist_features = [["Streams", "false"],
                             ["Lakes", "false"],
                             ["Roads", "false"]]

### MODEL CONFIGURATION ###

# Set basis functions
basis_functions = "LINEAR;QUADRATIC;PRODUCT;HINGE"
number_knots = 10

# Set the study area
study_area_type = "CONVEX_HULL"
study_area_polygon = None

# Set spatial thinning
spatial_thinning = "THINNING"
min_nearest_neighbor_distance = "500 Meters"
number_of_iterations = 10

# Set the relative weight of presence to background and link function, using
# background points as observed absence
relative_weight = 1
link_function = "LOGISTIC"

# Set the presence probability cutoff
cutoff = 0.3

### MODEL OUTPUTS AND VALIDATION ###

# Set training outputs for model evaluation
out_trained_features = "Out_Trained_Features"
out_trained_raster = "Out_Trained_Raster"
out_response_curve_table = "Out_Response_Curves"
out_sensitivity_table = "Out_Sensitivity_Table"

# Set cross-validation options
resampling_scheme = "RANDOM"
number_of_groups = 3

# Call the tool using the parameters defined above.
arcpy.stats.PresenceOnlyPrediction(
    input_point_features=in_point_features,
    contains_background=contains_background,
    explanatory_variables=explanatory_fields,
    explanatory_rasters=explanatory_rasters,
    distance_features=explanatory_dist_features,
    basis_expansion_functions=basis_functions,
    number_knots=number_knots,
    study_area_type=study_area_type,
    spatial_thinning=spatial_thinning,
    thinning_distance_band=min_nearest_neighbor_distance,
    number_of_iterations=number_of_iterations,
    relative_weight=relative_weight,
    link_function=link_function,
    presence_probability_cutoff=cutoff,
    output_trained_features=out_trained_features,
    output_trained_raster=out_trained_raster,
    output_response_curve_table=out_response_curve_table,
    output_sensitivity_table=out_sensitivity_table,
    resampling_scheme=resampling_scheme,
    number_of_groups=number_of_groups)

## 僅存在預測 (MaxEnt) (空間統計工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 空間統計工具箱
- 空間關系建模工具集

**english**　Presence-only Prediction (MaxEnt)

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/presence-only-prediction.html

**id**　49e61008e1c610d166-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-statistics/presence-only-prediction.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Presence-only Prediction (MaxEnt)，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入點圖徵 [input_point_features]、包含背景點 （可選） [contains_background]、存在指示器欄位 （可選） [presence_indicator_field]、解釋訓練變量 （可選） [explanatory_variables]、解釋訓練距離圖徵 （可選） [distance_features]、解釋訓練網格 （可選） [explanatory_rasters]、解釋變量擴展（基函數） （可選） [basis_expansion_functions]、節數 （可選） [number_knots]、研究區域 （可選） [study_area_type]、研究區域面 （可選） [study_area_polygon]、應用空間細化 （可選） [spatial_thinning]、最小最近鄰距離 （可選） [thinning_distance_band]、細化迭代次數 （可選） [number_of_iterations]、存在與背景的相對權重 （可選） [relative_weight]、存在概率變換（鏈接函數） （可選） [link_function]、存在概率中斷值 （可選） [presence_probability_cutoff]、輸出訓練圖徵 （可選） [output_trained_features]、輸出訓練網格 （可選） [output_trained_raster]、輸出響應曲線表 （可選） [output_response_curve_table]、輸出靈敏度表 （可選） [output_sensitivity_table]、輸入預測圖徵 （可選） [features_to_predict]、輸出預測圖徵 （可選） [output_pred_features]、輸出預測網格 （可選） [output_pred_raster]、匹配解釋變量 （可選） [explanatory_variable_matching]、匹配距離圖徵 （可選） [explanatory_distance_matching]、匹配解釋網格 （可選） [explanatory_rasters_matching]、允許資料范圍之外的預測 （可選） [allow_predictions_outside_of_data_ranges]、重采樣方案 （可選） [resampling_scheme]、組數 （可選） [number_of_groups]、輸出已訓練模型文件 （可選） [output_trained_model]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "input_point_features", "label_zh": "輸入點圖徵", "label_en": "Input Point Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The point features representing locations where presence of a phenomenon of interest is known to occur."}, {"parameter": "contains_background", "label_zh": "包含背景點 （可選）", "label_en": "Contains Background Points (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the input point features contain background points. If the input points do not contain background points, the tool will generate background points using cells in the explanatory training rasters. The tool uses background points to model the characteristics of the landscape in unknown locations and compare them to landscape characteristics in known presence locations. Therefore, background points can be considered as the study area. Generally, these are locations where presence of a phenomenon of interest is unknown. However, if any information is known about the background points, the Relative Weight of Presence to Background parameter can be used to indicate this. Checked—The input point features include background points. Unchecked—The input point features do not include background points. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "presence_indicator_field", "label_zh": "存在指示器欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Presence Indicator Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field from the input point features containing binary values that indicate each point as presence (1) or background (0). The field must be numeric."}, {"parameter": "explanatory_variables", "label_zh": "解釋訓練變量 （可選）", "label_en": "Explanatory Training Variables (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "A list of fields representing the explanatory variables that will help predict the probability of presence. You can specify whether each variable is categorical or numeric. Check the Categorical check box for each variable that represents a class or category (such as land cover). Value table columns: Variable—Fields representing the explanatory variables that will help predict the probability of presence. Categorical—Specifies whether the variable is categorial or numeric. Categorical—The explanatory variable is a categorical variable. Numeric—The explanatory variable is a continuous variable."}, {"parameter": "distance_features", "label_zh": "解釋訓練距離圖徵 （可選）", "label_en": "Explanatory Training Distance Features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "A list of feature layers or feature classes that will be used to automatically create explanatory variables that represent the distance from the input point features to the nearest provided distance features. If the input explanatory training distance features are polygons or lines, the distance attributes are calculated as the distance between the closest segment and the point."}, {"parameter": "explanatory_rasters", "label_zh": "解釋訓練網格 （可選）", "label_en": "Explanatory Training Rasters (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "A list of rasters that will be used to automatically create explanatory training variables in the model whose values are extracted from rasters. For each feature (presence and background points) in the input point features, the value of the raster cell will be extracted at that exact location. Bilinear raster resampling will be used when extracting the raster value for continuous rasters. Nearest neighbor assignment will be used when extracting a raster value from categorical rasters. You can specify whether each raster value is categorical or numeric. Check the Categorical check box for each raster that represents a class or category (such as land cover). Value table columns: Variable—The rasters that will be used to automatically create explanatory training variables in the model whose values are extracted from rasters. Categorical—Specifies whether each raster value is categorical or numeric. Categorical—The explanatory raster is a categorical variable. Numeric—The explanatory raster is a continuous variable."}, {"parameter": "basis_expansion_functions", "label_zh": "解釋變量擴展（基函數） （可選）", "label_en": "Explanatory Variable Expansions (Basis Functions) (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the basis function that will be used to transform the provided explanatory variables for use in the model. If multiple basis functions are selected, the tool will produce multiple transformed variables and attempt to use them in the model. Original (Linear)—A linear transformation to the input variables will be applied. This is the default Pairwise interaction (Product)—A pairwise multiplication on continuous explanatory variables will be used, yielding interaction variables. This option is only available when multiple explanatory variables have been provided. Smoothed step (Hinge)—The continuous explanatory variable values will be converted into two segments, a static segment (composed of zeroes or ones) and a linear function segment (increasing or decreasing). Discrete step (Threshold)—The continuous explanatory variable values will be converted into a binary variable composed of zeroes and ones. Squared (Quadratic)—The square of each continuous explanatory variable value will be returned."}, {"parameter": "number_knots", "label_zh": "節數 （可選）", "label_en": "Number of Knots (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The number of knots that will be used by the hinge and threshold explanatory variable expansions. The value controls how many thresholds are created, which are used to create multiple explanatory variable expansions using each threshold. The value must be between 2 and 50. The default is 10."}, {"parameter": "study_area_type", "label_zh": "研究區域 （可選）", "label_en": "Study Area (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the type of study area that will be used to define where presence is possible when the input point features do not contain background points. Convex hull—The smallest convex polygon that encloses all the presence points in the input point features will be used. This is the default Raster extent—The extent of the intersection of the explanatory training rasters will be used. Polygon study area—A custom study area that is defined by a polygon feature class will be used."}, {"parameter": "study_area_polygon", "label_zh": "研究區域面 （可選）", "label_en": "Study Area Polygon (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "A feature class containing the polygons that define a custom study area. The input point features must be located within the custom study area covered by the polygon features. A study area can be composed of multiple polygons."}, {"parameter": "spatial_thinning", "label_zh": "應用空間細化 （可選）", "label_en": "Apply Spatial Thinning (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether spatial thinning will be applied to presence and background points before training the model. Spatial thinning helps to reduce sampling bias by removing points and ensuring that remaining points have a minimum nearest-neighbor distance, set in the Minimum Nearest Neighbor Distance parameter. Spatial thinning is also applied to background points whether they are provided in input point features or generated by the tool. Checked—Spatial thinning will be applied. Unchecked—Spatial thinning will not be applied. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "thinning_distance_band", "label_zh": "最小最近鄰距離 （可選）", "label_en": "Minimum Nearest Neighbor Distance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The minimum distance between any two presence points or any two background points when spatial thinning is applied."}, {"parameter": "number_of_iterations", "label_zh": "細化迭代次數 （可選）", "label_en": "Number of Iterations for Thinning (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The number of runs that will be used to find the optimal spatial thinning solution, seeking to maintain as many presence and background points as possible while ensuring that no two presence or two background points are within the specified Minimum Nearest Neighbor Distance parameter value. The minimum possible is 1 iteration and the maximum possible is 50 iterations. The default is 10. This parameter is only applicable for spatial thinning applied to presence and background points in the input point features. Spatial thinning that is applied to background points generated from raster cells undergo spatial thinning by resampling the raster cells to the specified Minimum Nearest Distance parameter value without needing to iterate for an optimal solution."}, {"parameter": "relative_weight", "label_zh": "存在與背景的相對權重 （可選）", "label_en": "Relative Weight of Presence to Background (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "A value between 1 and 100 that specifies the relative information weight of presence points to background points. The default is 100. A higher value indicates that presence points are the primary source of information; it is unknown whether background points represent presence or absence and background points receive lower weight in the model. A lower value indicates that background points also contribute valuable information that can be used in conjunction with presence points; there is greater confidence that background points represent absence and their information can be used in the model as absence locations."}, {"parameter": "link_function", "label_zh": "存在概率變換（鏈接函數） （可選）", "label_en": "Presence Probability Transformation (Link Function) (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the function that will convert the unbounded outputs of the model to a number between 0 and 1. This value can be interpreted as the probability of presence at the location. Each option converts the same continuous value to a different probability. C-log-log—The C-log-log link function will be used to convert the predictions to probabilities. This option is recommended when the presence and location of a phenomenon is unambiguous, for example, when modeling the presence of an immobile plant species. This is the default. Logistic—The logistic link function will be used to convert predictions to probabilities. This option is recommended when the presence and location of a phenomenon is ambiguous, for example, when modeling the presence of a migratory animal species."}, {"parameter": "presence_probability_cutoff", "label_zh": "存在概率中斷值 （可選）", "label_en": "Presence Probability Cutoff (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "A cutoff value between 0.01 and 0.99 that establishes which probabilities correspond with presence in the resulting classification. The cutoff value is used to help evaluate the model's performance using training data and known presence points. Classification diagnostics are provided in geoprocessing messages and in the output trained features."}, {"parameter": "output_trained_features", "label_zh": "輸出訓練圖徵 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Trained Features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "An output feature class that will contain all features and explanatory variables used in the training of the model."}, {"parameter": "output_trained_raster", "label_zh": "輸出訓練網格 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Trained Raster (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Dataset", "description_en": "The output raster with cell values indicating the probability of presence using the selected link function. The default cell size is the maximum of the cell sizes of the explanatory training rasters. An output trained raster can only be created if the input point features do not contain background points."}, {"parameter": "output_response_curve_table", "label_zh": "輸出響應曲線表 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Response Curve Table (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Table", "description_en": "The output table that will contain diagnostics from the training model that indicate the effect of each explanatory variable on the probability of presence after accounting for the average effects of all other explanatory variables in the model. The table will have up to two derived charts of partial dependence plots: one set of line charts for continuous variables and one set of bar charts for categorical variables."}, {"parameter": "output_sensitivity_table", "label_zh": "輸出靈敏度表 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Sensitivity Table (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Table", "description_en": "The output table that will contain diagnostics of training model accuracy as the probability presence cutoff changes from 0 to 1."}, {"parameter": "features_to_predict", "label_zh": "輸入預測圖徵 （可選）", "label_en": "Input Prediction Features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The feature class representing locations where predictions will be made. The feature class must contain any provided explanatory variable fields that were used from the input point features. When using spatial thinning, you can use the original input point features as input prediction features to receive a prediction for the entire dataset."}, {"parameter": "output_pred_features", "label_zh": "輸出預測圖徵 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Prediction Features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "The output feature class that will contain the results of the prediction model applied to the input prediction features."}, {"parameter": "output_pred_raster", "label_zh": "輸出預測網格 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Prediction Raster (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Dataset", "description_en": "The output raster containing the prediction results at each cell of the matched explanatory rasters. The default cell size is the maximum of the cell sizes of the explanatory training rasters."}, {"parameter": "explanatory_variable_matching", "label_zh": "匹配解釋變量 （可選）", "label_en": "Match Explanatory Variables (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The matching explanatory variable fields for the input point features and input prediction features. Value table columns: Prediction—A list of variables that correspond to the training variables that will be made to make predictions. Training—The list of variables from the explanatory training variables used to train the model."}, {"parameter": "explanatory_distance_matching", "label_zh": "匹配距離圖徵 （可選）", "label_en": "Match Distance Features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The matching distance features for the training and prediction. Value table columns: Prediction—A list of features that correspond to the training distance features that will be made to make predictions. Training—The list of the distance features used to train the model."}, {"parameter": "explanatory_rasters_matching", "label_zh": "匹配解釋網格 （可選）", "label_en": "Match Explanatory Rasters (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The matching rasters for the training and prediction. Value table columns: Prediction—A list of rasters that correspond to the training rasters that will be made to make predictions. Training—The list rasters used to train the model."}, {"parameter": "allow_predictions_outside_of_data_ranges", "label_zh": "允許資料范圍之外的預測 （可選）", "label_en": "Allow Predictions Outside of Data Ranges (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the prediction will allow extrapolation when explanatory variable values are out of the range of values used in training. Checked—The prediction will allow extrapolation beyond the range of values used in training. This is the default. Unchecked—The prediction will not allow extrapolation beyond the range of values used in training."}, {"parameter": "resampling_scheme", "label_zh": "重采樣方案 （可選）", "label_en": "Resampling Scheme (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the method that will be used to perform cross validation of the prediction model. Cross validation excludes a portion of the data during training of the model and uses it to test the model's performance after it is trained. None—Cross validation will not be performed. This is the default Random—The points will be randomly divided into groups, and each group will be left out once when performing cross validation. The number of groups is specified in the Number of Groups parameter."}, {"parameter": "number_of_groups", "label_zh": "組數 （可選）", "label_en": "Number of Groups (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The number of groups that will be used in cross validation for the random resampling scheme. A field in the output trained features indicates the group that each point was assigned to. The default is 3. A minimum of 2 groups and a maximum of 10 groups are allowed."}, {"parameter": "output_trained_model", "label_zh": "輸出已訓練模型文件 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Trained Model File (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "File", "description_en": "An output model file that will save the trained model, which can be used later for prediction."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.stats.PresenceOnlyPrediction(input_point_features, {contains_background}, {presence_indicator_field}, {explanatory_variables}, {distance_features}, {explanatory_rasters}, {basis_expansion_functions}, {number_knots}, {study_area_type}, {study_area_polygon}, {spatial_thinning}, {thinning_distance_band}, {number_of_iterations}, {relative_weight}, {link_function}, {presence_probability_cutoff}, {output_trained_features}, {output_trained_raster}, {output_response_curve_table}, {output_sensitivity_table}, {features_to_predict}, {output_pred_features}, {output_pred_raster}, {explanatory_variable_matching}, {explanatory_distance_matching}, {explanatory_rasters_matching}, {allow_predictions_outside_of_data_ranges}, {resampling_scheme}, {number_of_groups}, {output_trained_model})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：input_point_features、contains_background、presence_indicator_field、explanatory_variables、distance_features、explanatory_rasters、basis_expansion_functions、number_knots、study_area_type、study_area_polygon、spatial_thinning、thinning_distance_band、number_of_iterations、relative_weight、link_function、presence_probability_cutoff、output_trained_features、output_trained_raster、output_response_curve_table、output_sensitivity_table、features_to_predict、output_pred_features、output_pred_raster、explanatory_variable_matching、explanatory_distance_matching、explanatory_rasters_matching、allow_predictions_outside_of_data_ranges、resampling_scheme、number_of_groups、output_trained_model。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 配置使用 LRS (遠程引用工具) 配置外部事件 · 使用率

**id**　49f241f6ba60617669-2

**english**　Configure External Event With LRS

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱
- 配置工具et
- LRS 事件工具

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/configure-external-event-with-lrs.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/configure-external-event-with-lrs.html

**text**　相關事件所在的地理資料庫和LRS所居住的地理資料庫必須是企業地理資料庫。 這個工具也可以用來修改現有外部已配置事件的名稱、 欄位和事件行為。 以下事件行為規則默認設定: 活動規則 校準路線 保持退役路線 保持延長路線 保持延長路線 保持重新指定路線 保持對地路線 保持反向路線 保持卡托 榮耀道路 措施

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The geodatabase where the associated events are present and the geodatabase in which the LRS resides must be enterprise geodatabases.\nThis tool can also be used to modify the name, fields, and event behaviors for the existing configured external events.\nThe following event behavior rules are set by default: Activity Rule Calibrate Route Stay Put Retire Route Stay Put Extend Route Stay Put Reassign Route Stay Put Realign Route Stay Put Reverse Route Stay Put Carto Realign Route Honor Route Measure", "section": "Usage"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/configure-external-event-with-lrs.html#usage

## 配置使用 LRS (遠程引用工具) 配置外部事件 · 參數

**id**　49f241f6ba60617669-19

**english**　Configure External Event With LRS

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱
- 配置工具et
- LRS 事件工具

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/configure-external-event-with-lrs.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/configure-external-event-with-lrs.html

**parameter**　calibrate_rule

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標簽標簽
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 校準規則(可選)
- 指定校準活動的事件行為規則。 保持原位—— 事件地理位置將被保留; 措施可能會改變。 這是默認的。 退休- 兩種測量和地理位置將會被保留; 事件將退出。 移動 - 活動的尺度將會被保留; 地理位置可能發生變化 。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["Calibrate Rule (Optional)", "Specifies the event behavior rule for the calibrate activity. Stay put—The geographic location of the event will be preserved; measures may change. This is the default. Retire—Both measure and geographic location will be preserved; the event will be retired. Move—The measures of the event will be preserved; the geographic location may change.", "String"], "section": "Parameters"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/configure-external-event-with-lrs.html#parameters

## 配置使用 LRS (遠程引用工具) 配置外部事件 · 參數

**id**　49f241f6ba60617669-20

**english**　Configure External Event With LRS

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱
- 配置工具et
- LRS 事件工具

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/configure-external-event-with-lrs.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/configure-external-event-with-lrs.html

**parameter**　retire_rule

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標簽標簽
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- (可選擇的)規則
- 指定退休活動的事件行為規則。 呆著別動—— 事件地理位置將保持不變; 措施可能改變。 這是默認的。 退休- 兩種計量和地理位置將予保留; 事件將予留存。 移動 - 事件的措施將予保留; 地理位置可能改變。 Snap - 事件地理位置將通過將事件按同一路線進行, 措施可能會改變 。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["Retire Rule (Optional)", "Specifies the event behavior rule for the retire activity. Stay put—The geographic location of the event will be preserved; measures may change. This is the default. Retire—Both measure and geographic location will be preserved; the event will be retired. Move—The measures of the event will be preserved; the geographic location may change. Snap—The geographic location of the event will be preserved by snapping the event to a concurrent route; measures may change.", "String"], "section": "Parameters"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/configure-external-event-with-lrs.html#parameters

## 配置使用 LRS (遠程引用工具) 配置外部事件 · 參數

**id**　49f241f6ba60617669-21

**english**　Configure External Event With LRS

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱
- 配置工具et
- LRS 事件工具

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/configure-external-event-with-lrs.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/configure-external-event-with-lrs.html

**parameter**　extend_rule

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標簽標簽
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 延長規則(可選擇)
- 指定延長活動的事件行為規則。 保持原位—— 事件地理位置將保留; 措施可能改變。 這是默認的。 退休- 兩種測量和地理位置將保存; 事件將退出。 移動 - 事件計量將保留; 地理位置可能會變化。 覆蓋 - 線性事件的幾何位置和度量將被修改, 以包含新的或新修改的部分 。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["Extend Rule (Optional)", "Specifies the event behavior rule for the extend activity. Stay put—The geographic location of the event will be preserved; measures may change. This is the default. Retire—Both measure and geographic location will be preserved; the event will be retired. Move—The measures of the event will be preserved; the geographic location may change. Cover—The geometric location and measure of a line event will be modified to include a new or newly modified section.", "String"], "section": "Parameters"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/configure-external-event-with-lrs.html#parameters

- 姓名名稱名稱名稱
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- (可選擇)
- 指定退休活動的事件行為規則。STAY_PUT - 事件地理位置將保持不變; 措施可能會改變。 這是默認的。 RETIRE - 兩種測量和地理位置都將被保留; 事件將會退役。 MOVE - 事件的措施將保持不變; 地理位置可能發生變化。 SNAP - 事件的地理位置將通過將事件切換到并行路線而得以保留; 措施可能會改變 。

This is the default. RETIRE—Both measure and geographic location will be preserved; the event will be retired. MOVE—The measures of the event will be preserved; the geographic location may change. SNAP—The geographic location of the event will be preserved by snapping the event to a concurrent route; measures may change.", "String"], "section": "Derived output"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/configure-external-event-with-lrs.html#derived-output

- 指定延長活動的事件行為規則。STAY_PUT - 將保留該活動的地理位置; 措施可能會改變。 這是默認的。 RETIRE - 兩種測量和地理位置都將保存; 事件將會被留存。 MOVE - 事件的措施將保留; 地理位置可能發生變化。 CEVER - 線性事件的幾何位置和尺度將被修改, 以包含新的或新修改的部分 。

# Tool variables
in_event = "C:\\Eventdata.sde\\pointevent"
parent_network = "C:\\Networkdata.sde\\Network1"
event_name = "PointEvent"
event_id_field = "EventId"
route_id_field = "RouteId"
measure_field =  "Measure"
geometry_type = "POINT"
to_measure_field = None

to_date_field =  "ToDate"

retire_rule = "STAY_PUT"

# run the tool
arcpy.lr.ConfigureExternalEventWithLRS(in_event, parent_network, event_name, event_id_field, route_id_field, measure_field, geometry_type, to_measure_field, from_date_field, to_date_field, event_spans_routes, to_route_id_field, store_route_name, route_name_field, to_route_name_field, calibrate_rule, retire_rule, extend_rule, reassign_rule, realign_rule, reverse_rule, carto_realign_rule)

route_name_field = "FromRouteName_updated"
to_route_name_field = "ToRouteName_updated"
calibrate_rule = "RETIRE"
retire_rule = "RETIRE"
extend_rule = "RETIRE"
reassign_rule = "RETIRE"
realign_rule = "RETIRE"
reverse_rule = "STAY_PUT"
carto_realign_rule = "HONOR_ROUTE_MEASURE"

# Set current workspace
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:\\Networkdata.sde"

# Run the tool
arcpy.lr.ConfigureExternalEventWithLRS(in_event, parent_network, event_name, event_id_field, route_id_field, measure_field, geometry_type, to_measure_field, from_date_field, to_date_field, event_spans_routes, to_route_id_field, store_route_name, route_name_field, to_route_name_field, calibrate_rule, retire_rule, extend_rule, reassign_rule, realign_rule, reverse_rule, carto_realign_rule)

**original**　{"text": "# Name: Configure_Externalevent_LRS_ex5.py\n# Description: Modify an existing external line event spanning routes of an LRS Network using a stand-alone Python script. Event name and optional parameter fields are updated.\n# Requires: ArcGIS Linear Referencing\n\n# Import arcpy module\nimport arcpy\n\n# Tool variables\nin_event = \"C:\\\\Eventdata.sde\\\\lineevent\"\nparent_network = \"C:\\\\Networkdata.sde\\\\Network1\"\nevent_name = \"LineSpanningEvent_updated\"\nevent_id_field = \"EventId_updated\"\nroute_id_field = \"FromRouteId_updated\"\nmeasure_field =  \"FromMeasure_updated\"\ngeometry_type = \"LINE\"\nto_measure_field = \"ToMeasure_updated\"\nfrom_date_field = \"FromDate_updated\"\nto_date_field = \"ToDate_updated\"\nevent_spans_routes = \"SPANS ROUTES\"\nto_route_id_field = \"ToRouteId_updated\"\nstore_route_name = \"STORE ROUTE NAME\"\nroute_name_field = \"FromRouteName_updated\"\nto_route_name_field = \"ToRouteName_updated\"\ncalibrate_rule = \"RETIRE\"\nretire_rule = \"RETIRE\"\nextend_rule = \"RETIRE\"\nreassign_rule = \"RETIRE\"\nrealign_rule = \"RETIRE\"\nreverse_rule = \"STAY_PUT\"\ncarto_realign_rule = \"HONOR_ROUTE_MEASURE\"\n\n# Set current workspace\narcpy.env.workspace = \"C:\\\\Networkdata.sde\"\n\n# Run the tool\narcpy.lr.ConfigureExternalEventWithLRS(in_event, parent_network, event_name, event_id_field, route_id_field, measure_field, geometry_type, to_measure_field, from_date_field, to_date_field, event_spans_routes, to_route_id_field, store_route_name, route_name_field, to_route_name_field, calibrate_rule, retire_rule, extend_rule, reassign_rule, realign_rule, reverse_rule, carto_realign_rule)", "section": "Code sample"}

**english**　Configure External Event With LRS

- 工具參考

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/configure-external-event-with-lrs.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_event", "label_zh": "投入活動", "label_en": "Input Event", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Table View", "description_en": "The external event feature class or table that will be registered to an LRS."}, {"parameter": "parent_network", "label_zh": "LRS 家長網絡", "label_en": "Parent LRS Network", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The LRS Network to which the event will be registered."}, {"parameter": "event_name", "label_zh": "LRS 事件名稱", "label_en": "LRS Event Name", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the external event or table that will be registered to the LRS."}, {"parameter": "event_id_field", "label_zh": "ID 實地活動", "label_en": "Event ID Field", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The event ID field available in the event feature class or table."}, {"parameter": "route_id_field", "label_zh": "線路ID外地", "label_en": "Route ID Field", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The name of the route ID field if it is a point event or a line event that does not span routes, or the name of the from route ID field if the event spans routes. The field must be available in the external event table or feature class."}, {"parameter": "measure_field", "label_zh": "計量欄位", "label_en": "Measure Field", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The name of the measure field if it is a point event or the name of the from measure field if it is a line event."}, {"parameter": "geometry_type", "label_zh": "幾何類型(可選)", "label_en": "Geometry Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the geometry type of the external event or table. Point—The geometry type of the event will be point. This is the default. Line—The geometry type of the event will be polyline."}, {"parameter": "to_measure_field", "label_zh": "測量欄位( 可選)", "label_en": "To Measure Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The name of the to measure field. This field is only required for line events."}, {"parameter": "from_date_field", "label_zh": "從日期開始( 可選)", "label_en": "From Date Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The from date field in the event feature class or table."}, {"parameter": "to_date_field", "label_zh": "至日期欄位(可選)", "label_en": "To Date Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The to date field in the event feature class or table."}, {"parameter": "event_spans_routes", "label_zh": "事件垃圾郵件路徑(可選)", "label_en": "Event Spans Routes (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies whether the event records will span routes. As is—The property will not change. This is the default. Do not span routes—The event records will not span routes. This is applicable to line events only. Spans routes—The event records will span routes. This is applicable to line events only."}, {"parameter": "to_route_id_field", "label_zh": "至路由ID欄位(可選)", "label_en": "To Route ID Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The name of the to route ID field available in the event feature class or table."}, {"parameter": "store_route_name", "label_zh": "存儲路由名稱( 可選)", "label_en": "Store Route Name (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies whether the route name will be stored with the event records. As is—The property will not change. This is the default. Do not store route name—The route name will not be stored with the event records. Store route name—The route name will be stored with the event records."}, {"parameter": "route_name_field", "label_zh": "路線名稱欄位( 可選)", "label_en": "Route Name Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The route name field if it is a point event or line event that does not span routes, or the name of the from route name field if the event spans routes. The field must be available in the external event table or feature class."}, {"parameter": "to_route_name_field", "label_zh": "到路線名稱欄位( 可選)", "label_en": "To Route Name Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The to route name field for line events that span routes. This parameter is required if the Store Route Name and Event Spans Routes parameters are set."}, {"parameter": "calibrate_rule", "label_zh": "校準規則(可選)", "label_en": "Calibrate Rule (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the event behavior rule for the calibrate activity. Stay put—The geographic location of the event will be preserved; measures may change. This is the default. Retire—Both measure and geographic location will be preserved; the event will be retired. Move—The measures of the event will be preserved; the geographic location may change."}, {"parameter": "retire_rule", "label_zh": "(可選擇的)規則", "label_en": "Retire Rule (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the event behavior rule for the retire activity. Stay put—The geographic location of the event will be preserved; measures may change. This is the default. Retire—Both measure and geographic location will be preserved; the event will be retired. Move—The measures of the event will be preserved; the geographic location may change. Snap—The geographic location of the event will be preserved by snapping the event to a concurrent route; measures may change."}, {"parameter": "extend_rule", "label_zh": "延長規則(可選擇)", "label_en": "Extend Rule (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the event behavior rule for the extend activity. Stay put—The geographic location of the event will be preserved; measures may change. This is the default. Retire—Both measure and geographic location will be preserved; the event will be retired. Move—The measures of the event will be preserved; the geographic location may change. Cover—The geometric location and measure of a line event will be modified to include a new or newly modified section."}, {"parameter": "reassign_rule", "label_zh": "重新指派規則( 可選)", "label_en": "Reassign Rule (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the event behavior rule for the reassign activity. Stay put—The geographic location of the event will be preserved; measures may change. This is the default. Retire—Both measure and geographic location will be preserved; the event will be retired. Move—The measures of the event will be preserved; the geographic location may change. Snap—The geographic location of the event will be preserved by snapping the event to a concurrent route; measures may change."}, {"parameter": "realign_rule", "label_zh": "調整規則(備選)", "label_en": "Realign Rule (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the event behavior rule for the realign activity. Stay put—The geographic location of the event will be preserved; measures may change. This is the default. Retire—Both measure and geographic location will be preserved; the event will be retired. Move—The measures of the event will be preserved; the geographic location may change. Snap—The geographic location of the event will be preserved by snapping the event to a concurrent route; measures may change. Cover—The geometric location and measure of a line event will be modified to include a new or newly modified section."}, {"parameter": "reverse_rule", "label_zh": "反向規則(可選擇)", "label_en": "Reverse Rule (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the event behavior rule for the reverse activity. Stay put—The geographic location of the event will be preserved; measures may change. This is the default. Retire—Both measure and geographic location will be preserved; the event will be retired. Move—The measures of the event will be preserved; the geographic location may change."}, {"parameter": "carto_realign_rule", "label_zh": "化裝調整規則(可選)", "label_en": "Carto Realign Rule (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the event behavior rule for the cartographic realign activity. Honor Route Measure—The measure of the event will be preserved or changed proportionally to the route's measure change. This is the default."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.lr.ConfigureExternalEventWithLRS(in_event, parent_network, event_name, event_id_field, route_id_field, measure_field, {geometry_type}, {to_measure_field}, {from_date_field}, {to_date_field}, {event_spans_routes}, {to_route_id_field}, {store_route_name}, {route_name_field}, {to_route_name_field}, {calibrate_rule}, {retire_rule}, {extend_rule}, {reassign_rule}, {realign_rule}, {reverse_rule}, {carto_realign_rule})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_event、parent_network、event_name、event_id_field、route_id_field、measure_field、geometry_type、to_measure_field、from_date_field、to_date_field、event_spans_routes、to_route_id_field、store_route_name、route_name_field、to_route_name_field、calibrate_rule、retire_rule、extend_rule、reassign_rule、realign_rule、reverse_rule、carto_realign_rule。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 配置使用 LRS (遠程引用工具) 配置外部事件 · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱
- 配置工具et
- LRS 事件工具

**english**　Configure External Event With LRS

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/configure-external-event-with-lrs.html

**id**　49f241f6ba60617669-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/configure-external-event-with-lrs.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Configure External Event With LRS，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。投入活動 [in_event]、LRS 家長網絡 [parent_network]、LRS 事件名稱 [event_name]、ID 實地活動 [event_id_field]、線路ID外地 [route_id_field]、計量欄位 [measure_field]、幾何類型(可選) [geometry_type]、測量欄位( 可選) [to_measure_field]、從日期開始( 可選) [from_date_field]、至日期欄位(可選) [to_date_field]、事件垃圾郵件路徑(可選) [event_spans_routes]、至路由ID欄位(可選) [to_route_id_field]、存儲路由名稱( 可選) [store_route_name]、路線名稱欄位( 可選) [route_name_field]、到路線名稱欄位( 可選) [to_route_name_field]、校準規則(可選) [calibrate_rule]、(可選擇的)規則 [retire_rule]、延長規則(可選擇) [extend_rule]、重新指派規則( 可選) [reassign_rule]、調整規則(備選) [realign_rule]、反向規則(可選擇) [reverse_rule]、化裝調整規則(可選) [carto_realign_rule]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/configure-external-event-with-lrs.html#parameters

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/modify-lrs.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/modify-lrs.html

**text**　此工具可用于具有或沒有幾何或記錄的特征類或表格中。 路徑 ID 和中線 ID 必須是獨特的; 但是, 該工具不檢查重復的 ID 。 此工具不檢查錯誤的網絡身份; 如果網絡身份不存在, 則被視為錯誤。 在 LRS 資料模型中了解更多關于配置 LRS 和欄位屬性的信息 。 ArcMap 所支持的事件行為 Realign 重疊規則在 ArcMap Pro 中不支持。 如果事件行為是使用 Realign 重疊規則在ArcMap 中配置的, 則將使用可比較的 Realign 事件行為值進行替換 。 此工具將檢查相沖突的 Realign 和 Realiming 重疊事件行為, 并將支持該程序的行為。如果遇到 Realign 重疊事件行為, 此工具不支持 Reprign 重疊事件行為。 注意: ArcMap 中支持的 Snap 選項在ArcGIS Pro 2. 9 或以后的 ArcGIS Pro 中支持 Remerign 事件行為。 下表列出了 ArcMap 中一個 LRS 中配置 Reforign 和 Real 重疊事件行為的結果值: 如果ArcMap 中的Realign 是ArcMap 中的 Realign Overlap 在ArcMap ArcMap 中是 River Put Stay 的Riest Put Stay Stay 繼續維持在“ 保持” Cast Stay Stay Stay Put 保持“ 保持“ 快速快動移動 移動 移動封面動作移動移動” 移動 Casting Fronte Friitee 覆蓋退休人員

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "This tool can be used on a feature class or table with or without geometry or records.\nThe route ID and centerline ID must be unique; however, the tool does not check for duplicate IDs.\nThis tool does not check for erroneous network IDs; if the network ID does not exist, it is considered erroneous. Learn more about configuring LRS and field properties in the LRS data model\nThe Realign Overlaps rule for event behavior supported in ArcMap is not supported in ArcGIS Pro. If event behaviors were configured in ArcMap using the Realign Overlaps rule, substitutions will be made using comparable Realign event behavior values. This tool will check for conflicting Realign and Realign Overlaps event behaviors and will support Realign event behavior if Realign Overlaps event behavior is encountered. This tool does not support Realign Overlaps event behavior. Note: The Snap option supported in ArcMap is supported for Realign event behavior in ArcGIS Pro 2.9 or later. The following table lists the resulting value in ArcGIS Pro for configured Realign and Realign Overlaps event behavior in an LRS migrated from ArcMap: If Realign in ArcMap is And Realign Overlaps in ArcMap is Realign in ArcGIS Pro will be Stay Put Move Stay Put Stay Put Retire Stay Put Stay Put Cover Stay Put Stay Put Snap Snap Move Stay Put Move Move Retire Move Move Cover Move Move Snap Snap Retire Stay Put Retire Retire Move Retire Retire Cover Retire Retire Snap Snap Cover Stay Put Cover Cover Move Cover Cover Retire Cover Cover Snap Cover", "section": "Usage"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/modify-lrs.html#usage

**id**　4a07ee491a76b3933e-29

**english**　Modify LRS

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標簽標簽
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 預防沖突(備選)
- 指定是否為輸入的LRS提供沖突預防。只有在編輯或進行地理處理時,才能使用作為特寫服務公布的分支版資料。和目前一樣,將使用目前的沖突預防設置。這是默認。輸入的 LRS將啟用啟用沖突預防。輸入的LRS將禁用Disable-沖突預防。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["Conflict Prevention (Optional)", "Specifies whether conflict prevention will be enabled for the input LRS. Conflict prevention is only available when editing or performing geoprocessing on branch versioned data that is published as a feature service. As is—The current conflict prevention setting will be used. This is the default. Enable—Conflict prevention will be enabled for the input LRS. Disable—Conflict prevention will be disabled for the input LRS.", "String"], "section": "Parameters"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/modify-lrs.html#parameters

**text**　# Name: Modify_LRS_ex1.py
# Description: Modifies an existing linear referencing system (LRS) in the specified workspace.
# Requires: ArcGIS Linear Referencing

# Import arcpy module
import arcpy

# Tool variables
input_workspace = r"c:\ModifyLRS\Py\ModifyLRS_python.gdb"
current_lrs_name = "LRS1"
new_lrs_name = "NewLRS"
centerline_feature_class = r"c:\ModifyLRS\Py\ModifyLRS_python.gdb\LRS\centerline1"
centerline_centerline_ID_field = "centerlineId"
centerline_sequence_table = r"c:\ModifyLRS\Py\ModifyLRS_python.gdb\LRS\centerline_Sequence1"
centerline_sequence_centerline_id_field = "centerlineId"
centerline_sequence_route_id_field = "RouteId"
centerline_sequence_from_date_field = "FromDate"
centerline_sequence_to_date_field = "ToDate"
centerline_sequence_network_ID_field = "NetworkId"
calibration_point_feature_class = r"c:\ModifyLRS\Py\ModifyLRS_python.gdb\LRS\calibration_Point1"
calibration_point_measure_field = "Measure"
calibration_point_from_date_field = "FromDate"
calibration_point_to_date_field = "ToDate"
calibration_point_route_id_field = "RouteId"
calibration_point_network_ID_field = "NetworkId"
redline_feature_class = r"c:\ModifyLRS\Py\ModifyLRS_python.gdb\LRS\Redline1"
redline_from_measure_field = "FromMeasure"
redline_to_measure_field = "ToMeasure"
redline_route_id_field = "RouteId"
redline_route_name_field = "RouteName"
redline_effective_date_field = "EffectiveDate"
redline_activity_type_field = "ActivityType"
redline_network_id_field = "NetworkId"
move_to_feature_dataset = "MOVE"

# Set current workspace
arcpy.env.workspace = r"c:\ModifyLRS\Py\ModifyLRS_python.gdb"

# Run the tool
arcpy.lr.ModifyLRS(in_workspace, current_lrs_name, new_lrs_name, centerline_feature_class, centerline_centerline_id_field, centerline_sequence_table, centerline_sequence_centerline_id_field, centerline_sequence_route_id_field, centerline_sequence_from_date_field, centerline_sequence_to_date_field, centerline_sequence_network_id_field, calibration_point_feature_class, calibration_point_measure_field, calibration_point_from_date_field, calibration_point_to_date_field, calibration_point_route_id_field, calibration_point_network_id_field, redline_feature_class, redline_from_measure_field, redline_to_measure_field, redline_route_id_field, redline_route_name_field, redline_effective_date_field, redline_activity_type_field, redline_network_id_field, move_to_feature_dataset)

**text**　# Name: Modify_LRS_ex2.py
# Description: Modify an existing linear referencing system (LRS) in the specified workspace.
# Requires: ArcGIS Linear Referencing

# Import arcpy module
import arcpy

# Local variables:
input_workspace = r"c:\ModifyLRS\Py\ModifyLRS_python.gdb"
current_lrs_name = "LRS1"
new_lrs_name = "NewLRS"
centerline_feature_class = r"c:\ModifyLRS\Py\ModifyLRS_python.gdb\LRS\centerline1"
centerline_centerline_ID_field = "centerlineId"
centerline_sequence_table = r"c:\ModifyLRS\Py\ModifyLRS_python.gdb\LRS\centerline_Sequence1"
centerline_sequence_centerline_id_field = "centerlineId"
centerline_sequence_route_id_field = "RouteId"
centerline_sequence_from_date_field = "FromDate"
centerline_sequence_to_date_field = "ToDate"
centerline_sequence_network_ID_field = "NetworkId"
calibration_point_feature_class = r"c:\ModifyLRS\Py\ModifyLRS_python.gdb\LRS\calibration_Point1"
calibration_point_measure_field = "Measure"
calibration_point_from_date_field = "FromDate"
calibration_point_to_date_field = "ToDate"
calibration_point_route_id_field = "RouteId"
calibration_point_network_ID_field = "NetworkId"
redline_feature_class = r"c:\ModifyLRS\Py\ModifyLRS_python.gdb\LRS\Redline1"
redline_from_measure_field = "FromMeasure"
redline_to_measure_field = "ToMeasure"
redline_route_id_field = "RouteId"
redline_route_name_field = "RouteName"
redline_effective_date_field = "EffectiveDate"
redline_activity_type_field = "ActivityType"
redline_network_id_field = "NetworkId"
move_to_feature_dataset = "MOVE"

# Set current workspace
arcpy.env.workspace = r"c:\ModifyLRS\Py\ModifyLRS_python.gdb"

# Run the tool
arcpy.lr.ModifyLRS(in_workspace, current_lrs_name, new_lrs_name, centerline_feature_class, centerline_centerline_id_field, centerline_sequence_table, centerline_sequence_centerline_id_field, centerline_sequence_route_id_field, centerline_sequence_from_date_field, centerline_sequence_to_date_field, centerline_sequence_network_id_field, calibration_point_feature_class, calibration_point_measure_field, calibration_point_from_date_field, calibration_point_to_date_field, calibration_point_route_id_field, calibration_point_network_id_field, redline_feature_class, redline_from_measure_field, redline_to_measure_field, redline_route_id_field, redline_route_name_field, redline_effective_date_field, redline_activity_type_field, redline_network_id_field, move_to_feature_dataset)

## 修改 LRS (遠程參考工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 引用工具箱
- 配置工具et

**english**　Modify LRS

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/modify-lrs.html

**id**　4a07ee491a76b3933e-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/linear-referencing/modify-lrs.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Modify LRS，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入工作空間 [in_workspace]、當前 LRS 名稱 [current_lrs_name]、新建 LRS 名稱( 可選) [new_lrs_name]、中心線 - 特色類( 可選) [centerline_feature_class]、中心線 - 中心線ID欄位( 可選) [centerline_centerline_id_field]、中線序列 - 表格( 可選) [centerline_sequence_table]、中線序列 - 中線 ID 欄位( 可選) [centerline_sequence_centerline_id_field]、中線序列 - 路徑標識欄位( 可選) [centerline_sequence_route_id_field]、中線序列 - 從日期欄位( 可選) [centerline_sequence_from_date_field]、中線序列 - 到日期欄位( 可選) [centerline_sequence_to_date_field]、中線序列 - 網絡ID欄位( 可選) [centerline_sequence_network_id_field]、校準點 - 特征類( 可選) [calibration_point_feature_class]、校準點 - 測量場(可選) [calibration_point_measure_field]、校準點 - 從日期欄位(可選) [calibration_point_from_date_field]、校準點 - 到日期欄位(可選) [calibration_point_to_date_field]、校準點 - 路線識別欄位(備選) [calibration_point_route_id_field]、校準點 - 網絡ID欄位( 可選) [calibration_point_network_id_field]、紅線 - 功能類( 可選) [redline_feature_class]、紅線 - 從測量欄位( 可選) [redline_from_measure_field]、紅線 - 測量欄位( 可選) [redline_to_measure_field]、紅線 - 路徑ID欄位(可選) [redline_route_id_field]、紅線 - 路線名稱欄位( 可選) [redline_route_name_field]、紅線 - 有效日期欄位(備選) [redline_effective_date_field]、紅線 - 活動類型欄位(備選) [redline_activity_type_field]、紅線 - 網絡ID欄位( 可選) [redline_network_id_field]、預防沖突(備選) [conflict_prevention]、將要求的功能分類移動到特性資料集( 可選) [move_to_feature_dataset]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/linear-referencing/modify-lrs.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/160001-170000/tool-errors-and-warnings-160051-160075-160051.html

**text**　雖然此錯誤可能會發生，但發生的頻率非常低，以至于我們尚且無法確定典型原因，也因此目前沒有可用的解決方案。
如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/160001-170000/tool-errors-and-warnings-160051-160075-160051.html#solution

## 構建網絡資料集圖層 (Network Analyst 工具) · 匯總

**id**　4a276e3b7678f4a178-1

**english**　Make Network Dataset Layer

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱
- 網絡資料集工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-network-dataset-layer.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-network-dataset-layer.html

**text**　從網絡資料集創建網絡資料集圖層。
每次將網絡資料集用作地理處理工具的輸入時，都將打開網絡資料集。 打開網絡資料集的運算量很大，因為資料集包含讀取和緩存的高級資料結構和表。 對于只打開資料集一次的網絡資料集圖層，它在后續工具中執行操作的效果會比重復使用網絡資料集要好。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-network-dataset-layer.html#summary

## 構建網絡資料集圖層 (Network Analyst 工具) · 用法

**id**　4a276e3b7678f4a178-2

**english**　Make Network Dataset Layer

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱
- 網絡資料集工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-network-dataset-layer.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-network-dataset-layer.html

**text**　網絡資料集圖層可用于任何將網絡資料集作為輸入的工作流。
由于此工具創建的網絡資料集圖層是臨時圖層，因此如果不加以保存，該圖層將在會話結束后消失。 要將該圖層保存到磁盤，請運行 保存至圖層文件 工具。
您可以通過此工具創建的網絡資料集圖層，使用 應用圖層的符號設置 工具從現有網絡資料集圖層文件導入符號系統。
要繪制的網絡元素參數的選項取決于網絡資料集所支持的網絡元素。 例如，如果網絡資料集不支持交通流量和轉彎，則這些選項將不可用。
在 Python 腳本的循環中執行網絡分析時，請在循環外部創建網絡資料集圖層，然后在循環內執行分析以提高性能。
使用地理處理服務執行網絡分析時，如果網絡資料集由目錄路徑引用，則當每次在服務內運行地理處理工具時都會打開該網絡資料集。 通過使用網絡資料集圖層而不是通過目錄路徑進行引用會有性能上的優勢，因為發布地理處理服務之后，在服務啟動時會打開資料集一次，還會對連接屬性進行緩存。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-network-dataset-layer.html#usage

## 構建網絡資料集圖層 (Network Analyst 工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Network Analyst 工具箱
- 網絡資料集工具集

**english**　Make Network Dataset Layer

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-network-dataset-layer.html

**id**　4a276e3b7678f4a178-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-network-dataset-layer.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Make Network Dataset Layer，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入網絡資料集 [in_network_dataset]、輸出圖層 [output_layer]、要繪制的網絡元素 （可選） [draw_elements]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/network-analyst/make-network-dataset-layer.html#parameters

如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

## 存儲容量 (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 匯總

**id**　4a3c83c653678501b6-1

**english**　Storage Capacity

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 水文分析工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/storage-capacity.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/storage-capacity.html

**text**　將為輸入表面網格創建高程及其相應存儲容量的表格和圖表。 該工具將計算一系列高程增量處基礎區域的表面面積和總體積。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/storage-capacity.html#summary

## 存儲容量 (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 用法

**id**　4a3c83c653678501b6-2

**english**　Storage Capacity

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 水文分析工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/storage-capacity.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/storage-capacity.html

**text**　該工具會針對一系列高程增量創建一張包含表面積和總體積的表。 該表包含每個區域中每個高程增量的記錄。 輸出表的模式如下： 欄位包括 ELEVATION、AREA 和 VOLUME。 面積和體積欄位包含每個高程增量下的存儲容量計算結果。 高程和體積欄位以 z 單位進行計算。 面積欄位以輸入表面網格的 x,y 單位進行計算。 未指定區域時，將為所有記錄添加一個值為 1 的 ZONE_CODE 欄位，并將分析范圍作為單個區域。 如果指定了區域，則根據區域欄位數值（Python 中的 zone_field）添加欄位。 對于整型區域欄位，輸出表中將添加同名欄位。 對于字符串區域欄位，輸出表中將添加同名欄位，并額外添加一個 ZONE_CODE 欄位。
可以選擇創建兩個圖表，用于繪制高程-面積曲線和高程-體積曲線。 圖表將與輸出表一起添加到內容窗格中。 圖表的命名約定為 &lt;out_chart> Elevation Area Curve 和 &lt;out_chart> Elevation Volume Curve，其中 &lt;out_chart> 會被替換為輸出圖表名稱參數（Python 中的 out_chart）中指定的名稱。 在創建包含多個區域的圖表時，圖表中的每條線代表一個區域。 可使用圖例在圖表中以交互方式開啟或關閉特定的線條。 有關在 ArcGIS Pro 中使用圖表的詳細信息，請參閱圖表快速瀏覽。 圖表只能在 ArcGIS Pro 中創建；獨立腳本不支持圖表。
使用分析類型參數（Python 中的 analysis_type）來選擇表面積、體積計算或兩者。 這決定了表和圖表的模式。 例如，如果選擇了面積選項（Python 中的 AREA），則輸出表中僅創建面積欄位，體積欄位將被忽略。 同樣，只會為面積-高程曲線創建一個圖表。
如果未指定區域，則將分析范圍作為單個區域來計算庫容。 指定可選的輸入網格或圖徵區域資料參數值（Python 中的 in_zone_data），代表需要計算庫容的區域。 例如，區域可以是一個水庫或一個滯洪池。 該工具支持跨多個區域的計算，且庫容是根據區域欄位在每個區域中獨立計算的。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/storage-capacity.html#usage

## 存儲容量 (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 用法

**id**　4a3c83c653678501b6-3

**english**　Storage Capacity

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 水文分析工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/storage-capacity.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/storage-capacity.html

**text**　計算庫容的高程增量基于最小高程（Python 中的 min_elevation）、最大高程（Python 中的 max_elevation）、增量類型（Python 中的 increment_type）和增量（Python 中的 increment）參數值。
默認的最小高程和最大高程參數值確定如下： 工具使用輸入表面網格中的區域最小值和最大值作為默認值。 這些值可能因區域而異，具體取決于輸入表面網格在這些區域中的最小值和最大值。 如果您指定了最小高程或最大高程參數值，則工具將在所有區域中使用相同的最小和最大增量值。 在這種情況下，如果高程增量的子集變得小于區域最小值，則面積和體積計算為零。 如果增量大于區域最大值，則不會計算這些增量的面積和體積，并視表格式顯示為空值或零。
指定 Z 單位（Python 中的 z_unit）參數值以指示輸入表面網格的垂直單位。 如果輸入表面網格指定了垂直座標系，則這些單位將作為默認 Z 單位。 如果未指定 Z 單位且輸入表面網格沒有垂直座標系，則默認 Z 單位為米。 當 X,Y 單位與 Z 單位不同時，計算容積時會將 X,Y 單位轉換為與 Z 單位匹配。
默認情況下，如果有多核處理器可用，此工具將使用多核處理。 最多可以使用 4 個核。 要使用較少的核，請使用并行處理因子環境設置。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/storage-capacity.html#usage

## 存儲容量 (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　4a3c83c653678501b6-31

**english**　Storage Capacity

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 水文分析工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/storage-capacity.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/storage-capacity.html

**text**　# Name: StorageCapacity_Standalone.py
# Description:
# Requirements: Spatial Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy.sa import *

# Set the analysis environments
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/arcpyExamples/data"

# Set local variables
in_surface_raster = "in_surface.tif"
out_table = "fgdb.gdb\out_table"
in_zones = "fgdb.gdb\in_zones"
zone_field = "zone_id"

# Run StorageCapacity tool
arcpy.sa.StorageCapacity(in_surface_raster, out_table, in_zones, zone_field)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/storage-capacity.html#parameters

## 存儲容量 (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 水文分析工具集

**english**　Storage Capacity

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/storage-capacity.html

**id**　4a3c83c653678501b6-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/storage-capacity.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Storage Capacity，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入表面網格 [in_surface_raster]、輸出表 [out_table]、輸入網格或圖徵區域資料 [in_zone_data]、區域欄位 （可選） [zone_field]、分析類型 （可選） [analysis_type]、最小高程 （可選） [min_elevation]、最大高程 （可選） [max_elevation]、增量類型 （可選） [increment_type]、遞增 （可選） [increment]、Z 單位 （可選） [z_unit]、輸出圖表名稱 （可選） [out_chart]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/storage-capacity.html#parameters

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/storage-capacity.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/storage-capacity.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_surface_raster", "label_zh": "輸入表面網格", "label_en": "Input surface raster", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Raster Layer", "description_en": "The input raster representing a continuous surface."}, {"parameter": "out_table", "label_zh": "輸出表", "label_en": "Output table", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Table", "description_en": "The output table that contains for each zone the surface area and total volumes for each increment in elevation."}, {"parameter": "in_zone_data", "label_zh": "輸入網格或圖徵區域資料", "label_en": "Input raster or feature zone data (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Layer; Feature Layer", "description_en": "The dataset that defines the zones. The zones can be defined by an integer raster or a feature layer."}, {"parameter": "zone_field", "label_zh": "區域欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Zone field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field that contains the values that define each zone. It can be an integer or a string field of the zone dataset."}, {"parameter": "analysis_type", "label_zh": "分析類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Analysis type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the analysis type. Area and Volume—Both surface areas and total volumes are calculated at each elevation increment. This is the default. Area—Surface area is calculated at each elevation increment. Volume—Total volume is calculated at each elevation increment."}, {"parameter": "min_elevation", "label_zh": "最小高程 （可選）", "label_en": "Minimum elevation (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The minimum elevation from which storage capacities are assessed. By default, the tool uses the minimum surface raster value in each zone as the minimum elevation for that zone. If a value is provided, it is used as the minimum elevation across all zones."}, {"parameter": "max_elevation", "label_zh": "最大高程 （可選）", "label_en": "Maximum elevation (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The maximum elevation from which storage capacities are assessed. By default, the tool uses the maximum surface raster value in each zone as the maximum elevation for that zone. If a value is provided, it is used as the maximum elevation across all zones."}, {"parameter": "increment_type", "label_zh": "增量類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Increment type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the increment type to use when computing elevation increments between minimum and maximum elevations. Number of Increments—The number of increments between minimum and maximum elevations is used. This is the default. Value of Increment—The elevation difference between each increment is used."}, {"parameter": "increment", "label_zh": "遞增 （可選）", "label_en": "Increment (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "An incremental value that is either the number of increments or the difference in elevation between increments. The value is determined based on the increment type parameter value."}, {"parameter": "z_unit", "label_zh": "Z 單位 （可選）", "label_en": "Z unit (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the linear unit that will be used for vertical z-values. Inch—The linear unit will be inches. Foot—The linear unit will be feet. US Foot—The linear unit will be US survey feet. Yard—The linear unit will be yards. US Mile—The linear unit will be US survey miles. Nautical mile—The linear unit will be nautical miles. Millimeter—The linear unit will be millimeters. Centimeter—The linear unit will be centimeters. Meter—The linear unit will be meters. Kilometer—The linear unit will be kilometers. Decimeter—The linear unit will be decimeters."}, {"parameter": "out_chart", "label_zh": "輸出圖表名稱 （可選）", "label_en": "Output chart name (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the output chart for display. The chart is listed in the Contents pane under Standalone Tables."}]

**signatures**　
- StorageCapacity(in_surface_raster, out_table, {in_zone_data}, {zone_field}, {analysis_type}, {min_elevation}, {max_elevation}, {increment_type}, {increment}, {z_unit}, {out_chart})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_surface_raster、out_table、in_zone_data、zone_field、analysis_type、min_elevation、max_elevation、increment_type、increment、z_unit、out_chart。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 并行處理因子 (環境設置) · 并行處理因子 (環境設置)

**id**　4a4f1fb3c81e756e2f-1

**english**　Parallel Processing Factor

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 環境
- 并行處理

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/environment-settings/parallel-processing-factor.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/environment-settings/parallel-processing-factor.html

**text**　采用并行處理因子環境的工具將跨多個進程劃分和執行操作。
許多現代計算機包含多核 CPU。 跨多個進程分散地理處理操作時，可通過使用多核提高性能。 并行處理的性能優勢因工具而異。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/environment-settings/parallel-processing-factor.html

## 并行處理因子 (環境設置) · 注:

**id**　4a4f1fb3c81e756e2f-2

**english**　Parallel Processing Factor

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 環境
- 并行處理

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/environment-settings/parallel-processing-factor.html#usage-notes

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/environment-settings/parallel-processing-factor.html

**text**　可利用單個工具修改該環境的使用方法。 有關任何取代以下信息的信息，請參閱工具文檔。
環境值決定了工具操作時所跨邏輯進程的數量。 這些邏輯進程將在計算機中的核心硬件之間進行分隔。 核心硬件的數量不隨該設置的改變而改變。
支持該環境的各工具均已針對特定計算機給出了邏輯進程數量的內置默認值。 您可基于資料、操作和可用資源對其進行更改。
如果您指定一個百分比值（用符號 % 代表），則所用的進程數量將取計算機內核數的指定百分數（取最近的整數）。 例如，在四核計算機上，設置 50% 意味著此操作將跨 2 個進程 (50% * 4 = 2)，設置 66% 意味著此操作將跨 3 個進程（66% * 4 = 2.64，四舍五入到 3），設置 100% 意味著此操作將跨所有 4 個進程 (100% * 4 = 4)。
使用 SQL Server Express 資料庫執行地理處理時存在一些限制。 SQL Server Express 允許一次最多建立三個連接。 每個處理中的 CPU 都需要連接到服務器。 此外，運行工具的軟件（如 ArcGIS Desktop）也將計作一個連接進程，因此，僅剩下兩個工作連接進程用于并行處理。
指定的進程數量大于計算機所具有的內核數，可能導致性能損失。 這是因為多進程將在一個核上爭奪資源。 要將環境指定為避免此類競爭，可以使用小于 100% 的百分數值或小于計算機內核數的進程數。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/environment-settings/parallel-processing-factor.html#usage-notes

## 并行處理因子 (環境設置) · 對話框語法

**id**　4a4f1fb3c81e756e2f-3

**english**　Parallel Processing Factor

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 環境
- 并行處理

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/environment-settings/parallel-processing-factor.html#dialog-syntax

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/environment-settings/parallel-processing-factor.html

**text**　并行處理因子 - 工具進行操作所跨進程數量。
blank (empty)—各工具決定要使用的進程數量。 這是默認設置。
0—不跨多個進程進行操作。
n—使用指定的進程數量。 此值將限制為計算機上處理單元的數量。 如果您指定的值大于計算機上的可用內核數，則并行處理實例的數量將限制為計算機上的可用內核數。
n%—使用指定百分比計算進程數量：Number of processes = number of system cores * n / 100。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/environment-settings/parallel-processing-factor.html#dialog-syntax
